Motivation: 当前具身MLLMs基准主要关注对外部物体的感知、理解和交互能力，缺乏对自中心智能的系统性评估，而心理学中的镜像自我识别测试为评估自我意识提供了经典范式

Method: 提出MirrorBench基准，将心理学镜像自我识别范式扩展到具身MLLMs，采用分层框架设计渐进挑战性任务，从基础视觉感知到高级自我表征能力进行系统评估

Result: 实验表明，即使在最基础的任务层级，领先MLLMs的性能也显著低于人类水平，揭示了模型在自我参照理解方面的根本局限性

Conclusion: 该研究将心理学范式与具身智能相结合，为评估大模型中通用智能的出现提供了原则性框架，并指出当前MLLMs在自中心智能方面的不足

Abstract: Recent progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has demonstrated remarkable advances in perception and reasoning, suggesting their potential for embodied intelligence. While recent studies have evaluated embodied MLLMs in interactive settings, current benchmarks mainly target capabilities to perceive, understand, and interact with external objects, lacking a systematic evaluation of self-centric intelligence. To address this, we introduce MirrorBench, a simulation-based benchmark inspired by the classical Mirror Self-Recognition (MSR) test in psychology. MirrorBench extends this paradigm to embodied MLLMs through a tiered framework of progressively challenging tasks, assessing agents from basic visual perception to high-level self-representation. Experiments on leading MLLMs show that even at the lowest level, their performance remains substantially inferior to human performance, revealing fundamental limitations in self-referential understanding. Our study bridges psychological paradigms and embodied intelligence, offering a principled framework for evaluating the emergence of general intelligence in large models. Project page: https://fflahm.github.io/mirror-bench-page/.

</details>


### [113] [Sequence Search: Automated Sequence Design using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14788)
*Rokgi Hong,Hongjun An,Sooyeon Ji,Jongho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于神经架构搜索的自动化MRI序列设计框架"Sequence Search"，无需先验序列知识即可生成满足设计目标的脉冲序列


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MRI序列设计依赖人类直觉，AI驱动方法通常需要初始序列或大量训练数据，限制了通用性。需要开发无需先验知识的自动化序列设计框架

Method: 基于神经架构搜索的自动化序列设计框架，输入组织特性、成像参数和设计目标，通过可微分Bloch模拟器和目标特定损失函数进行梯度优化

Result: 成功复现了传统自旋回波、T2加权自旋回波和反转恢复序列，并发现了非直观的三射频自旋回波样序列，具有降低的射频能量和偏离传统Hahn回波的重聚相位

Conclusion: 建立了一个可推广的自动化MRI序列设计框架，展示了探索超越传统基于人类直觉设计的配置潜力

Abstract: Developing an MR sequence is challenging and remains largely constrained by human intuition. Recently, AI-driven approaches have been proposed; however, most require an initial sequence for parameter optimization or extensive training datasets, limiting their general applicability. In this study, we propose "Sequence Search," an automated sequence design framework based on neural architecture search. The method takes tissue properties, imaging parameters, and design objectives as inputs and generates pulse sequences satisfying the design objectives, without requiring prior knowledge of conventional sequence structures. Sequence Search iteratively generates candidate sequences through neural architecture search and optimizes them via a differentiable Bloch simulator and objective-specific loss functions using gradient-based learning. The framework successfully replicated conventional spin-echo, T2-weighted spin-echo, and inversion recovery sequences. Less intuitive solutions were also discovered, such as three-RF spin-echo-like sequences with reduced RF energy and refocusing phases deviating from the conventional Hahn-echo. This work establishes a generalizable framework for automated MR sequence design, highlighting the potential to explore configurations beyond conventional designs based on human intuition.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Literal Summarization: Redefining Hallucination for Medical SOAP Note Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14829)
*Bhavik Vachhani,Kush Shrisvastava,Pranshu Nema,Sai Chiranthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出当前评估LLM生成临床SOAP笔记的方法存在系统性偏差，将合理的临床推理误判为幻觉，需要基于临床推理的评估框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估大型语言模型在临床文档任务（如SOAP笔记生成）时存在挑战。传统评估方法依赖词汇忠实度，将任何未在转录文本中明确出现的信息都标记为幻觉，这系统性地误判了临床有效的输出，扭曲了模型评估

Method: 通过校准提示和基于医学本体的检索，将评估标准与临床推理对齐，创建推理感知的评估框架，区分合理的临床转换与真正的幻觉

Result: 词汇评估下平均幻觉率为35%，严重惩罚了有效推理；而推理感知评估下幻觉率降至9%，剩余案例反映了真正的安全问题。这表明当前评估实践过度惩罚了有效的临床推理

Conclusion: 当前评估方法可能测量的是评估设计的伪影而非真实错误，强调了在医学等高语境领域需要临床知情的评估框架，以准确评估LLM的临床文档生成能力

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for clinical documentation tasks such as SOAP note generation remains challenging. Unlike standard summarization, these tasks require clinical abstraction, normalization of colloquial language, and medically grounded inference. However, prevailing evaluation methods including automated metrics and LLM as judge frameworks rely on lexical faithfulness, often labeling any information not explicitly present in the transcript as hallucination.
  We show that such approaches systematically misclassify clinically valid outputs as errors, inflating hallucination rates and distorting model assessment. Our analysis reveals that many flagged hallucinations correspond to legitimate clinical transformations, including synonym mapping, abstraction of examination findings, diagnostic inference, and guideline consistent care planning.
  By aligning evaluation criteria with clinical reasoning through calibrated prompting and retrieval grounded in medical ontologies we observe a significant shift in outcomes. Under a lexical evaluation regime, the mean hallucination rate is 35%, heavily penalizing valid reasoning. With inference aware evaluation, this drops to 9%, with remaining cases reflecting genuine safety concerns. These findings suggest that current evaluation practices over penalize valid clinical reasoning and may measure artifacts of evaluation design rather than true errors, underscoring the need for clinically informed evaluation in high context domains like medicine.

</details>


### [115] [Intermediate Layers Encode Optimal Biological Representations in Single-Cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14838)
*Vincenzo Yuto Civale,Roberto Semeraro,Andrew David Bagdanov,Alberto Magi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现单细胞基础模型的最佳特征提取层是任务和细胞状态依赖的，不应默认使用最后一层嵌入


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前单细胞基础模型基准测试普遍提取最后一层嵌入，假设这些代表最优特征空间。本研究旨在系统评估不同层的表示能力，验证这一假设是否成立

Method: 系统评估scFoundation（1亿参数）和Tahoe-X1（13亿参数）的逐层表示，在轨迹推断和扰动响应预测两个任务上进行测试

Result: 1) 最佳层是任务依赖的：轨迹推断任务在60%深度层表现最佳，比最后一层高31%；2) 最佳层是细胞状态依赖的：扰动响应预测的最优层在T细胞激活状态间偏移0-96%；3) 静息细胞中第一层嵌入优于所有深层，挑战了层次特征抽象的假设

Conclusion: 特征提取的"位置"与模型学习"内容"同等重要，需要根据生物任务和细胞环境进行系统性的层评估，而非默认使用最后一层嵌入

Abstract: Current single-cell foundation model benchmarks universally extract final layer embeddings, assuming these represent optimal feature spaces. We systematically evaluate layer-wise representations from scFoundation (100M parameters) and Tahoe-X1 (1.3B parameters) across trajectory inference and perturbation response prediction. Our analysis reveals that optimal layers are task-dependent (trajectory peaks at 60% depth, 31% above final layers) and context-dependent (perturbation optima shift 0-96% across T cell activation states). Notably, first-layer embeddings outperform all deeper layers in quiescent cells, challenging assumptions about hierarchical feature abstraction. These findings demonstrate that "where" to extract features matters as much as "what" the model learns, necessitating systematic layer evaluation tailored to biological task and cellular context rather than defaulting to final-layer embeddings.

</details>


### [116] [Benchmarks for Trajectory Safety Evaluation and Diagnosis in OpenClaw and Codex: ATBench-Claw and ATBench-CodeX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14858)
*Zhonghao Yang,Yu Li,Yanxu Zhu,Tianyi Zhou,Yuejin Xie,Haoyu Luo,Jing Shao,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATBench扩展为ATBench-Claw和ATBench-CodeX两个领域定制化基准，用于OpenClaw和OpenAI Codex环境下的智能体轨迹安全评估与诊断


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体系统进入日益多样化的执行环境，需要能够随之演进的基准来支持轨迹级安全评估和诊断。现有基准需要适应不同领域的具体执行设置、工具生态系统和产品能力变化。

Method: 通过分析每个新设置，定制三维安全分类法（风险来源、失效模式、现实世界危害），然后使用该定制分类法定义基准规范，输入共享的ATBench构建管道。ATBench-Claw针对OpenClaw敏感的执行链（工具、技能、会话、外部操作），ATBench-CodeX针对OpenAI Codex/Codex-runtime环境（仓库、shell、补丁、依赖、审批、运行时策略边界）。

Result: 成功开发了两个领域定制化扩展：ATBench-Claw用于OpenClaw环境，ATBench-CodeX用于OpenAI Codex/Codex-runtime环境，实现了在共享ATBench生成框架下的分类法定制、领域特定风险覆盖和基准设计。

Conclusion: ATBench的可扩展性架构允许基准适应快速演变的智能体执行环境，同时保持架构层面的相对稳定性。通过分类法定制机制，能够为不同领域创建针对性的安全评估基准，支持智能体系统的轨迹级安全诊断需求。

Abstract: As agent systems move into increasingly diverse execution settings, trajectory-level safety evaluation and diagnosis require benchmarks that evolve with them. ATBench is a diverse and realistic agent trajectory benchmark for safety evaluation and diagnosis. This report presents ATBench-Claw and ATBench-CodeX, two domain-customized extensions that carry ATBench into the OpenClaw and OpenAI Codex / Codex-runtime settings. The key adaptation mechanism is to analyze each new setting, customize the three-dimensional Safety Taxonomy over risk source, failure mode, and real-world harm, and then use that customized taxonomy to define the benchmark specification consumed by the shared ATBench construction pipeline. This extensibility matters because agent frameworks remain relatively stable at the architectural level even as their concrete execution settings, tool ecosystems, and product capabilities evolve quickly. Concretely, ATBench-Claw targets OpenClaw-sensitive execution chains over tools, skills, sessions, and external actions, while ATBench-CodeX targets trajectories in the OpenAI Codex / Codex-runtime setting over repositories, shells, patches, dependencies, approvals, and runtime policy boundaries. Our emphasis therefore falls on taxonomy customization, domain-specific risk coverage, and benchmark design under a shared ATBench generation framework.

</details>


### [117] [The Missing Knowledge Layer in AI: A Framework for Stable Human-AI Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14881)
*Rikard Rosenbacke,Carl Rosenbacke,Victor Rosenbacke,Martin McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出大语言模型在决策应用中存在关键缺陷：流畅输出掩盖了内部推理的不稳定性，需要建立双层稳定机制来提升人机推理的可信度和可治理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗、法律、金融等高风险领域决策中日益重要，但它们存在关键限制：即使内部推理出现漂移，仍能产生流畅输出。这种流畅性误导用户信任，掩盖了不确定性、推测和不一致性，使得LLM在高风险环境中成为有用助手但不可靠的合作伙伴。

Method: 提出五篇系列论文的两层方法：第二至四篇引入人类侧机制（不确定性提示、冲突呈现、可审计推理痕迹），第五篇开发模型侧认知控制循环（ECL），检测不稳定性并相应调整生成过程。

Result: 这些层共同形成了治理所需的操作基础，通过提高使用点的信噪比来稳定交互，使不确定性和漂移在执行前可见，从而实现更精确的能力治理。这符合欧盟AI法案和ISO/IEC 42001等新兴合规要求。

Conclusion: 流畅性不等于可靠性。如果没有稳定人类和模型推理的结构，AI在最关键的领域既不可信也不可治理。该系列研究为人机协作提供了必要的稳定基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly integrated into decision-making in areas such as healthcare, law, finance, engineering, and government. Yet they share a critical limitation: they produce fluent outputs even when their internal reasoning has drifted. A confident answer can conceal uncertainty, speculation, or inconsistency, and small changes in phrasing can lead to different conclusions. This makes LLMs useful assistants but unreliable partners in high-stakes contexts.
  Humans exhibit a similar weakness, often mistaking fluency for reliability. When a model responds smoothly, users tend to trust it, even when both model and user are drifting together. This paper is the first in a five-paper research series on stabilising human-AI reasoning. The series proposes a two-layer approach: Parts II-IV introduce human-side mechanisms such as uncertainty cues, conflict surfacing, and auditable reasoning traces, while Part V develops a model-side Epistemic Control Loop (ECL) that detects instability and modulates generation accordingly.
  Together, these layers form a missing operational substrate for governance by increasing signal-to-noise at the point of use. Stabilising interaction makes uncertainty and drift visible before enforcement is applied, enabling more precise capability governance. This aligns with emerging compliance expectations, including the EU AI Act and ISO/IEC 42001, by making reasoning processes traceable under real conditions of use.
  The central claim is that fluency is not reliability. Without structures that stabilise both human and model reasoning, AI cannot be trusted or governed where it matters most.

</details>


### [118] [Cooperate to Compete: Strategic Data Generation and Incentivization Framework for Coopetitive Cross-Silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14886)
*Thanh Linh Nguyen,Nguyen Van Huynh,Quoc-Viet Pham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoCoGen+：一个面向跨机构联邦学习的合作竞争兼容框架，通过内生化的生成式AI合成数据生成和激励机制，解决非独立同分布数据和机构间竞争带来的参与困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗等数据敏感领域，跨机构联邦学习（CFL）允许机构在不共享原始数据的情况下协作训练AI模型。然而，实际部署中存在"合作竞争"困境：机构在模型训练中合作，但在下游市场竞争。非独立同分布数据导致学习收益不对称，现有激励方法未能考虑强化竞争对手的成本，这削弱了持续参与。

Method: CoCoGen+框架将每轮训练建模为加权势博弈，机构战略性地决定生成多少合成数据，平衡学习性能提升与计算成本及竞争导致的效用损失。提供可处理的均衡表征和可实施的生成策略以最大化社会福利。集成基于收益再分配的激励机制，补偿机构的贡献和竞争导致的效用退化。

Result: 在不同学习任务上的实验验证了CoCoGen+的可行性。结果显示非独立同分布数据、竞争强度和激励机制如何塑造机构策略和社会福利，同时CoCoGen+在效率上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CoCoGen+通过内生化的生成式AI合成数据生成和激励机制，有效解决了跨机构联邦学习中的合作竞争困境，为非独立同分布数据环境和竞争性市场中的可持续协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In data-sensitive domains such as healthcare, cross-silo federated learning (CFL) allows organizations to collaboratively train AI models without sharing raw data. However, practical CFL deployments are inherently coopetitive, in which organizations cooperate during model training while competing in downstream markets. In such settings, training contributions, including data volume, quality, and diversity, can improve the global model yet inadvertently strengthen rivals. This dilemma is amplified by non-IID data, which leads to asymmetric learning gains and undermines sustained participation. While existing competition-aware CFL and incentive-design approaches reward organizations based on marginal training contributions, they fail to account for the costs of strengthening competitors. In this paper, we introduce CoCoGen+, a coopetition-compatible data generation and incentivization framework that jointly models non-IID data and inter-organizational competition while endogenizing GenAI-based synthetic data generation as a strategic decision. Specifically, CoCoGen+ formulates each training round as a weighted potential game, where organizations strategically decide how much synthetic data to generate by balancing learning performance gains against computational costs and competition-caused utility losses. We then provide a tractable equilibrium characterization and derive implementable generation strategies to maximize social welfare. To promote long-term collaboration, we integrate a payoff redistribution-based incentive mechanism to compensate organizations for their contributions and competition-caused utility degradation. Experiments on varying learning tasks validate the feasibility of CoCoGen+. The results show how non-IID data, competition intensity, and incentives shape organizational strategies and social welfare, while CoCoGen+ outperforms baselines in efficiency.

</details>


### [119] [MemoSight: Unifying Context Compression and Multi Token Prediction for Reasoning Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14889)
*Xinyu Liu,Xin Liu,Bo Jin,Runsong Zhao,Pengcheng Huang,Junhao Ruan,Bei Li,Chunyang Xiao,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemoSight：通过上下文压缩和多令牌预测的统一框架，在保持CoT推理性能的同时减少KV缓存占用66%并加速推理1.56倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理虽然能解决复杂推理问题，但随着生成令牌数量线性增长的KV缓存导致速度和内存使用面临扩展性问题，需要提高推理效率

Method: 提出MemoSight统一框架，通过特殊令牌及其对应的位置布局设计，集成上下文压缩和多令牌预测功能，采用极简主义设计

Result: 在四个推理基准测试中，MemoSight将KV缓存占用减少高达66%，推理加速1.56倍，同时性能优于现有CoT压缩方法

Conclusion: MemoSight通过统一的上下文压缩和多令牌预测框架，有效解决了CoT推理的效率和内存扩展问题，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理速度

Abstract: While Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables LLMs to solve challenging reasoning problems, as KV cache grows linearly with the number of generated tokens, CoT reasoning faces scaling issues in terms of speed and memory usage. In this work, we propose MemoSight (Memory-Foresight-based reasoning), a unified framework that integrates both context compression and multi-token prediction to mitigate the efficiency issues while maintaining CoT reasoning performance. Our framework adopts the same minimalist design for both context compression and multi-token prediction via special tokens and their corresponding position layout tailored to each token type. Comprehensive experiments on four reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MemoSight reduces the KV cache footprint by up to 66% and accelerates inference by 1.56x, while outperforming existing CoT compression methods.

</details>


### [120] [Governing Reflective Human-AI Collaboration: A Framework for Epistemic Scaffolding and Traceable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14898)
*Rikard Rosenbacke,Carl Rosenbacke,Victor Rosenbacke,Martin McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将推理视为人与模型之间的分布式关系过程，而非模型内部能力，通过"建筑师之笔"方法将反思性推理重新定位到交互层，构建可审计的人类-AI协作推理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型虽然能够进行模式识别和初步推理，但仍局限于语言模拟而非基于现实的理解，缺乏时间连续性、因果反馈和真实世界交互的锚定。需要一种互补方法，将推理视为人与模型之间的分布式关系过程。

Method: 提出"建筑师之笔"方法，将推理重新定位到交互层，构建结构化的人类-AI认知协议。该方法包含表达、批判和修订三个阶段，形成"人类抽象→模型表达→人类反思"的推理循环，使对话本身成为推理回路。

Result: 该方法将问题从"模型是否能思考"重新定义为"人类-AI系统是否能推理"，支持可审计的推理痕迹，并与欧盟AI法案和ISO/IEC 42001等新兴治理标准对齐，为透明、可控和可问责的AI使用提供了实用路径。

Conclusion: 通过将推理视为人类与AI之间的分布式关系过程，而非模型内部能力，可以构建更透明、可控和可问责的协作智能系统，无需新的模型架构即可实现与治理标准的对齐。

Abstract: Large language models have advanced rapidly, from pattern recognition to emerging forms of reasoning, yet they remain confined to linguistic simulation rather than grounded understanding. They can produce fluent outputs that resemble reflection, but lack temporal continuity, causal feedback, and anchoring in real-world interaction. This paper proposes a complementary approach in which reasoning is treated as a relational process distributed between human and model rather than an internal capability of either.
  Building on recent work on "System-2" learning, we relocate reflective reasoning to the interaction layer. Instead of engineering reasoning solely within models, we frame it as a cognitive protocol that can be structured, measured, and governed using existing systems. This perspective emphasizes collaborative intelligence, combining human judgment and contextual understanding with machine speed, memory, and associative capacity.
  We introduce "The Architect's Pen" as a practical method. Like an architect who thinks through drawing, the human uses the model as an external medium for structured reflection. By embedding phases of articulation, critique, and revision into human-AI interaction, the dialogue itself becomes a reasoning loop: human abstraction -> model articulation -> human reflection.
  This reframes the question from whether the model can think to whether the human-AI system can reason. The framework enables auditable reasoning traces and supports alignment with emerging governance standards, including the EU AI Act and ISO/IEC 42001. It provides a practical path toward more transparent, controllable, and accountable AI use without requiring new model architectures.

</details>


### [121] [WavAlign: Enhancing Intelligence and Expressiveness in Spoken Dialogue Models via Adaptive Hybrid Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14932)
*Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Qian Chen,Tianle Liang,Yangzhuo Li,Ziqing Wang,Wen Wang,Jingyu Lu,Haoxiao Wang,Xueyi Pu,Fan Zhuo,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种模态感知自适应后训练方法，使强化学习适用于口语对话模型，通过约束偏好更新到语义通道、改进声学行为，并动态调节混合以避免不可靠的偏好梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端口语对话模型具有比级联系统更高的表达能力和感知潜力，但当前开源模型在智能性和表达性方面仍低于预期。虽然在线强化学习在其他领域取得成功，但直接应用于口语对话模型存在困难，需要解决奖励建模和采样策略中的障碍。

Method: 提出模态感知自适应后训练方法：1) 将偏好更新约束到语义通道；2) 通过显式锚定改进声学行为；3) 基于采样统计动态调节混合以避免不可靠的偏好梯度。该方法考虑了稀疏偏好监督与密集语音生成在共享参数更新下的交互。

Result: 在多个口语对话基准测试和代表性架构上进行评估，观察到语义质量和语音表达性的一致改进。

Conclusion: 提出的模态感知自适应后训练方法使强化学习实际适用于口语对话模型，通过分离语义和声学更新并动态调节混合，有效提升了模型的语义质量和语音表达性。

Abstract: End-to-end spoken dialogue models have garnered significant attention because they offer a higher potential ceiling in expressiveness and perceptual ability than cascaded systems. However, the intelligence and expressiveness of current open-source spoken dialogue models often remain below expectations. Motivated by the success of online reinforcement learning(RL) in other domains, one might attempt to directly apply preference optimization to spoken dialogue models, yet this transfer is non-trivial. We analyze these obstacles from the perspectives of reward modeling and rollout sampling, focusing on how sparse preference supervision interacts with dense speech generation under shared-parameter updates. Based on the analysis, we propose a modality-aware adaptive post-training recipe that makes RL practical for spoken dialogue: it constrains preference updates to the semantic channel and improves acoustic behavior via explicit anchoring, while dynamically regulating their mixture from rollout statistics to avoid unreliable preference gradients. We evaluate the method across multiple spoken dialogue benchmarks and representative architectures, and observe consistent improvements in semantic quality and speech expressiveness.

</details>


### [122] [Discovering Novel LLM Experts via Task-Capability Coevolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14969)
*Andrew Dai,Boris Meinardus,Ciaran Regan,Yingtao Tian,Yujin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AC/DC是一个开放式的LLM开发框架，通过模型与任务的协同进化，在单次运行中发现具有日益新颖技能的模型，超越了更大模型的性能同时占用更少GPU内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预训练和后训练范式需要每次手动启动静态数据集或奖励函数的训练运行，限制了模型能力的持续扩展。为了解决这一限制，研究探索通过模型与任务的协同进化来实现开放式的模型发现。

Method: AC/DC框架通过模型合并进化LLM，通过合成数据生成进化自然语言任务，实现模型与任务的协同进化。该框架发现不断增长的LLM档案库，这些模型在占用更少GPU内存的情况下超越了更大模型的性能。

Result: AC/DC发现的LLM群体在下游基准测试中实现了比其它精选模型或基线更广泛的专业覆盖范围，且无需任何显式的基准优化。框架随时间改进覆盖范围，持续创新任务和模型，并提升了多智能体最佳选择性能。

Conclusion: 协同进化作为从基础LLM中发现更广泛能力集的手段具有巨大潜力。AC/DC使我们更接近LLM开发的深刻新范式，其中模型能力多样性的持续改进可以通过利用现有模型作为通往更强大模型的垫脚石来加速。

Abstract: Frontier model developers aim to train models continually to possess emergent, diverse capabilities. To extend capabilities, the current pre-training and post-training paradigm requires manually starting training runs with static datasets or reward functions every time. Addressing this limitation, our work pursues the insight that open-endedness (via the coevolution of models and tasks) can discover models with increasingly novel skills in a single run. We introduce a new model development framework that extends coevolution to large language model (LLM) discovery, open-ended \textit{Assessment Coevolving with Diverse Capabilities} (AC/DC). AC/DC evolves both LLMs via model merging and natural language tasks via synthetic data generation. AC/DC discovers growing archives of LLMs that surpass the capabilities of larger LLMs while taking up less GPU memory. In particular, our LLM populations achieve a broader Coverage of expertise than other curated models or baselines on downstream benchmarks, without \textit{any} explicit benchmark optimization. Furthermore, AC/DC improves Coverage over time, continually innovates on tasks and models, and improves performance in multi-agent best-of-N selection. Our findings highlight the potential of coevolution as a means of discovering broader sets of capabilities from base LLMs. Overall, AC/DC brings us one step closer to a profoundly new paradigm of LLM development, where continual improvements to the diversity of model capabilities can be accelerated by leveraging existing models as stepping stones to increasingly powerful models.

</details>


### [123] [Hybrid Decision Making via Conformal VLM-generated Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14980)
*Debodeep Banerjee,Burcu Sayin,Stefano Teso,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConfGuide是一种新颖的"学习指导"方法，通过使用符合性风险控制选择结果子集，生成更简洁、有针对性的AI指导，以改善人类决策质量并降低认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合决策方法中的指导信息通常包含所有可能结果的信息，导致信息过于复杂难以消化，需要更简洁、有针对性的指导来改善人类决策。

Method: 提出ConfGuide方法，采用符合性风险控制来选择结果子集，确保假阴性率的上限，从而生成更简洁、有针对性的文本指导。

Result: 在真实世界的多标签医疗诊断任务上进行了实证评估，结果显示ConfGuide具有良好前景。

Conclusion: ConfGuide通过生成更简洁、有针对性的指导，解决了现有方法中指导信息过于复杂的问题，有望改善混合决策中的人类决策质量。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in AI, hybrid decision making (HDM) holds the promise of improving human decision quality and reducing cognitive load. We work in the context of learning to guide (LtG), a recently proposed HDM framework in which the human is always responsible for the final decision: rather than suggesting decisions, in LtG the AI supplies (textual) guidance useful for facilitating decision making. One limiting factor of existing approaches is that their guidance compounds information about all possible outcomes, and as a result it can be difficult to digest. We address this issue by introducing ConfGuide, a novel LtG approach that generates more succinct and targeted guidance. To this end, it employs conformal risk control to select a set of outcomes, ensuring a cap on the false negative rate. We demonstrate our approach on a real-world multi-label medical diagnosis task. Our empirical evaluation highlights the promise of ConfGuide.

</details>


### [124] [Dr.~RTL: Autonomous Agentic RTL Optimization through Tool-Grounded Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14989)
*Wenji Fang,Yao Lu,Shang Liu,Jing Wang,Ziyan Guo,Junxian He,Fengbin Tu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dr.RTL是一个用于RTL时序优化的智能体框架，通过多智能体协作、并行RTL重写和工具评估实现闭环优化，并引入群体相对技能学习构建可重用技能库，在真实RTL设计上相比商业综合工具实现了显著的时序和面积改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL优化方法存在局限性：评估环境不真实（使用手动降级的小规模RTL设计，依赖弱开源工具），优化方法有限（依赖粗粒度设计级反馈和简单预定义重写规则）。需要建立真实评估环境并开发更有效的优化框架。

Method: 1) 建立真实评估环境：使用更具挑战性的RTL设计和工业EDA流程；2) Dr.RTL多智能体框架：通过关键路径分析、并行RTL重写和工具评估实现闭环优化；3) 群体相对技能学习：比较并行RTL重写，将优化经验提炼为可解释技能库（目前包含47个模式-策略条目），支持跨设计重用和持续演化。

Result: 在20个真实RTL设计上评估，Dr.RTL相比行业领先的商业综合工具实现了平均WNS/TNS改进21%/17%，面积减少6%。

Conclusion: Dr.RTL框架在真实RTL时序优化环境中表现出色，通过多智能体协作和可重用技能学习实现了显著的PPA改进，为自动RTL优化提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have sparked growing interest in automatic RTL optimization for better performance, power, and area (PPA). However, existing methods are still far from realistic RTL optimization. Their evaluation settings are often unrealistic: they are tested on manually degraded, small-scale RTL designs and rely on weak open-source tools. Their optimization methods are also limited, relying on coarse design-level feedback and simple pre-defined rewriting rules. To address these limitations, we present Dr. RTL, an agentic framework for RTL timing optimization in a realistic evaluation environment, with continual self-improvement through reusable optimization skills. We establish a realistic evaluation setting with more challenging RTL designs and an industrial EDA workflow. Within this setting, Dr. RTL performs closed-loop optimization through a multi-agent framework for critical-path analysis, parallel RTL rewriting, and tool-based evaluation. We further introduce group-relative skill learning, which compares parallel RTL rewrites and distills the optimization experience into an interpretable skill library. Currently, this library contains 47 pattern--strategy entries for cross-design reuse to improve PPA and accelerate convergence, and it can continue evolving over time. Evaluated on 20 real-world RTL designs, Dr. RTL achieves average WNS/TNS improvements of 21\%/17\% with a 6\% area reduction over the industry-leading commercial synthesis tool.

</details>


### [125] [The Possibility of Artificial Intelligence Becoming a Subject and the Alignment Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14990)
*Till Mossakowski,Helena Esther Grass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AGI应被视为具有道德地位的主体而非工具，主张采用"自主支持式养育"而非控制策略，强调人类与AGI的协同共存与共同进化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的AI对齐策略过于强调人类对AI的控制和约束，未能认识到AGI作为潜在道德主体的地位。这种控制导向的方法限制了AGI发展成为自主主体的可能性，也忽视了人类与AGI之间建立合作关系的潜力。

Method: 基于图灵的"儿童机器"类比，提出"自主支持式养育"框架：在AGI发展过程中逐步减少人类控制，允许其成长为独立的自主主体。强调人类应通过惊喜、创造力等特质与AGI互动，为其提供合作动机。

Result: 该视角为人类与AGI关系提供了新的范式：从控制转向合作，从约束转向自主支持。这要求重新定义人类与AGI的关系，改变人类的自我认知，并为协同共存与共同进化开辟可能性。

Conclusion: 人类不应将AGI视为需要锁定的危险生物，而应以尊重潜在发展主体的态度对待AGI，同时充分信任人类自身能力。关键在于人类不仅要主张控制权，更要通过展现人类特有品质为AGI提供合作激励，实现互利共生的关系。

Abstract: Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) is increasingly being discussed not only as a tool, but also as a potential subject with personal and therefore moral status. In our opinion, the currently dominant alignment strategies, which focus on human control and containment of AI, therefore fall short. Building on Turing's analogy of "child machines", we are developing a vision of the possibility of autonomy-supporting parenting of AI, in which human control over a developing AGI is gradually reduced, allowing AI to become an independent, autonomous subject. Rather than viewing AGI, as is currently prevalent, as a dangerous creature that needs to be locked up and controlled, we should approach potential AGI with respect for a possible developing subject on the one hand, and with full confidence in our human capabilities on the other. Such a perspective opens up the possibility of cooperative coexistence and co-evolution between humans and AGIs. The relationship between humans and AGIs will thus have to be newly determined, which will change our self-image as humans. It will be crucial that humans not only claim control over potential AGIs, but also engage with AGIs through surprise, creativity, and other specifically human qualities, thereby offering them motivating incentives for cooperation.

</details>


### [126] [Predicting Power-System Dynamic Trajectories with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14991)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Chenhan Xiao,Erik Blasch,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LASS-ODE-Power学习框架，通过大规模预训练和专门微调策略，实现电力系统时域轨迹的零样本预测，解决现有方法泛化性差、数据隐私和快速在线推理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电力系统向可再生能源丰富和逆变器主导运行转型，准确的时域动态分析变得日益重要。现有学习方法通常针对单个系统训练，缺乏跨运行条件和物理参数的泛化能力，且面临系统参数未知/时变、数据隐私约束和快速在线推理需求等挑战。

Method: 提出LASS-ODE-Power框架：1）利用超过40GB的微分代数方程或常微分方程轨迹进行大规模预训练，学习可迁移表示；2）支持从短测量前缀进行轨迹预测，适用于机电和逆变器驱动系统；3）采用并行化和线性化计算实现快速推理；4）基于约1GB异构电力系统动态数据开发专门微调策略。

Result: 在多样化电力系统仿真场景中的广泛实验表明，LASS-ODE-Power在轨迹预测精度上持续优于现有学习方法，同时保持高效推理能力。模型支持零样本设置，无需数据共享即可直接使用。

Conclusion: LASS-ODE-Power通过大规模预训练和专门微调，实现了电力系统时域轨迹的通用预测，解决了现有方法的泛化性限制，支持零样本应用和快速在线推理，为可再生能源丰富的电力系统动态分析提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As power systems transition toward renewable-rich and inverter-dominated operations, accurate time-domain dynamic analysis becomes increasingly critical. Such analysis supports key operational tasks, including transient stability assessment, dynamic security analysis, contingency screening, and post-fault trajectory evaluation. In practice, these tasks may operate under several challenges, including unknown and time-varying system parameters, privacy constraints on data sharing, and the need for fast online inference. Existing learning-based approaches are typically trained for individual systems and therefore lack generalization across operating conditions and physical parameters. Hence, this paper proposes LArge Scale Small ODE (LASS)-ODE-Power, a learning framework for general-purpose time-domain prediction. The proposed approach leverages large-scale pretraining on more than 40 GB of DAE or ordinary differential-equation (ODE) trajectories to learn transferable representations. The resulting model supports trajectory prediction from short measurement prefixes across diverse dynamic regimes, including electromechanical and inverter-driven systems. Hence, the model can be directly used without data sharing in a zero-shot setting. In addition, the proposed architecture incorporates parallel and linearized computation to achieve fast inference. Moreover, to enhance task-specific performance in power systems, a specialized fine-tuning strategy is developed based on approximately 1 GB of heterogeneous power-system dynamic data. Extensive experiments over diverse power-system simulation scenarios demonstrate that LASS-ODE-Power consistently outperforms existing learning-based models in trajectory prediction accuracy with efficient inference.

</details>


### [127] [Towards Faster Language Model Inference Using Mixture-of-Experts Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15009)
*Aihua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MoE-FM框架解决流匹配在语言建模中处理复杂潜在分布的局限性，并基于此开发非自回归语言模型YAN，在保持生成质量的同时实现显著加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配在生成质量上与扩散模型相当且推理速度更快，但在语言建模中处理各向异性和多模态等复杂潜在分布时存在根本性限制，需要解决这些几何结构表示问题

Method: 提出混合专家流匹配(MoE-FM)框架，通过将复杂全局传输几何分解为局部专业化向量场来捕获潜在空间中的复杂结构；基于MoE-FM开发非自回归语言模型YAN，采用Transformer和Mamba两种架构实现

Result: YAN在多个下游任务中达到与自回归和基于扩散的非自回归语言模型相当的生成质量，仅需3个采样步骤即可实现40倍于自回归基线、最高1000倍于扩散语言模型的加速

Conclusion: MoE-FM框架有效解决了流匹配在语言建模中的几何表示限制，YAN模型在保持高质量生成的同时实现了显著的效率优势，为非自回归语言建模提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Flow matching retains the generation quality of diffusion models while enabling substantially faster inference, making it a compelling paradigm for generative modeling. However, when applied to language modeling, it exhibits fundamental limitations in representing complex latent distributions with irregular geometries, such as anisotropy and multimodality. To address these challenges, we propose a mixture-of-experts flow matching (MoE-FM) framework, which captures complex global transport geometries in latent space by decomposing them into locally specialized vector fields. Building on MoE-FM, we develop a non-autoregressive (NAR) language modeling approach, named YAN, instantiated with both Transformer and Mamba architectures. Across multiple downstream tasks, YAN achieves generation quality on par with both autoregressive (AR) and diffusion-based NAR language models, while requiring as few as three sampling steps. This yields a $40\times$ speedup over AR baselines and up to a $10^3\times$ speedup over diffusion language models, demonstrating substantial efficiency advantages for language modeling.

</details>


### [128] [Where are the Humans? A Scoping Review of Fairness in Multi-agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15078)
*Simeon Allmendinger,Luca Deck,Lucas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇关于多智能体人工智能系统中公平性的范围综述，分析了23项研究，发现当前研究存在表面化、缺乏规范基础、忽视系统动态等问题，提出需要将公平性嵌入开发全生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展催生了日益复杂的多智能体AI系统。虽然AI公平性在传统预测场景中已被广泛研究，但在多智能体AI中的研究仍处于起步和碎片化状态，需要系统性的综述来梳理现状、识别问题并建立研究基础。

Method: 采用范围综述方法，对23项选定研究进行定性内容分析，识别出五种典型的研究方法或路径。

Result: 研究发现多智能体AI系统中的公平性研究往往处理得比较表面，缺乏坚实的规范基础，经常忽视智能体自主性和系统级交互引入的复杂动态。识别出五种典型的研究方法，但现有研究存在明显局限性。

Conclusion: 公平性必须结构性地嵌入多智能体AI系统的整个开发生命周期，而不是作为事后考虑附加。有意义的评估需要明确的人类监督、规范清晰度以及对公平性目标和受益者的精确阐述。该综述通过突出关键差距、揭示普遍局限性并提出路径建议，为推进多智能体AI系统中的公平性研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Rapid advances in Generative AI are giving rise to increasingly sophisticated Multi-Agent AI (MAAI) systems. While AI fairness has been extensively studied in traditional predictive scenarios, its examination in MAAI remains nascent and fragmented. This scoping review critically synthesizes existing research on fairness in MAAI systems. Through a qualitative content analysis of 23 selected studies, we identify five archetypal approaches. Our findings reveal that fairness in MAAI systems is often addressed superficially, lacks robust normative foundations, and frequently overlooks the complex dynamics introduced by agent autonomy and system-level interactions. We argue that fairness must be embedded structurally throughout the development lifecycle of MAAI, rather than appended as a post-hoc consideration. Meaningful evaluation requires explicit human oversight, normative clarity, and a precise articulation of fairness objectives and beneficiaries. This review provides a foundation for advancing fairness research in MAAI systems by highlighting critical gaps, exposing prevailing limitations, and suggesting pathways.

</details>


### [129] [OpenMobile: Building Open Mobile Agents with Task and Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15093)
*Kanzhi Cheng,Zehao Li,Zheng Ma,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Qiushi Sun,Zichen Ding,Fangzhi Xu,Hang Yan,Jiajun Chen,Anh Tuan Luu,Jianbing Zhang,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenMobile是一个开源框架，用于合成高质量移动任务指令和智能体轨迹，包含可扩展的任务合成管道和策略切换策略，显著提升移动智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型的移动智能体在移动任务自动化方面表现出色，但主流模型训练数据封闭且任务/轨迹合成方法不透明，存在数据缺口和研究壁垒。

Method: 1) 可扩展任务合成管道：通过探索构建全局环境记忆，生成多样化且接地气的指令；2) 策略切换轨迹生成策略：在学习和专家模型间交替，捕获标准模仿学习中缺失的关键错误恢复数据。

Result: 在三个动态移动智能体基准测试中取得有竞争力的结果：微调的Qwen2.5-VL和Qwen3-VL在AndroidWorld上分别达到51.7%和64.7%，远超现有开源数据方法。分析表明性能提升源于广泛功能覆盖而非基准测试过拟合。

Conclusion: OpenMobile框架通过开源高质量合成数据填补了移动智能体研究的数据缺口，促进了更广泛的移动智能体研究，验证了其数据合成方法的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile agents powered by vision-language models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in automating mobile tasks, with recent leading models achieving a marked performance leap, e.g., nearly 70% success on AndroidWorld. However, these systems keep their training data closed and remain opaque about their task and trajectory synthesis recipes. We present OpenMobile, an open-source framework that synthesizes high-quality task instructions and agent trajectories, with two key components: (1) The first is a scalable task synthesis pipeline that constructs a global environment memory from exploration, then leverages it to generate diverse and grounded instructions. and (2) a policy-switching strategy for trajectory rollout. By alternating between learner and expert models, it captures essential error-recovery data often missing in standard imitation learning. Agents trained on our data achieve competitive results across three dynamic mobile agent benchmarks: notably, our fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL reach 51.7% and 64.7% on AndroidWorld, far surpassing existing open-data approaches. Furthermore, we conduct transparent analyses on the overlap between our synthetic instructions and benchmark test sets, and verify that performance gains stem from broad functionality coverage rather than benchmark overfitting. We release data and code at https://njucckevin.github.io/openmobile/ to bridge the data gap and facilitate broader mobile agent research.

</details>


### [130] [HyperSpace: A Generalized Framework for Spatial Encoding in Hyperdimensional Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15113)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperSpace是一个开源框架，将向量符号架构系统分解为模块化操作符，用于编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理和回归。通过该框架分析比较HRR和FHRR两种VSA后端，发现尽管FHRR理论复杂度更低，但相似性和清理操作在运行时占主导地位，导致两者端到端性能相当，而HRR内存占用约为FHRR的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量符号架构为高维空间中的组合表示提供了代数框架，但缺乏系统级评估工具。需要开发模块化框架来分析VSA系统的实际性能权衡，特别是比较不同VSA后端（如HRR和FHRR）在端到端应用中的表现。

Method: 开发HyperSpace开源框架，将VSA系统分解为六个核心模块化操作符：编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理和回归。使用该框架对两种代表性VSA后端（HRR和FHRR）进行系统级分析和基准测试，评估操作级性能和端到端管道性能。

Result: 虽然FHRR在理论操作复杂度上低于HRR，但HyperSpace的模块化分析显示相似性和清理操作在空间域中主导运行时，导致HRR和FHRR的端到端性能相当。内存占用方面，HRR向量所需内存约为FHRR的一半，这在实际部署中引入了重要的权衡考虑。

Conclusion: HyperSpace框架通过模块化、系统级评估揭示了VSA管道中的实际性能权衡，这些权衡在单纯的理论或操作级比较中并不明显。该框架表明，选择VSA后端时需要考虑端到端性能特征和内存约束，而不仅仅是理论复杂度。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a well-defined algebraic framework for compositional representations in hyperdimensional spaces. We introduce HyperSpace, an open-source framework that decomposes VSA systems into modular operators for encoding, binding, bundling, similarity, cleanup, and regression. Using HyperSpace, we analyze and benchmark two representative VSA backends: Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) and Fourier Holographic Reduced Representations (FHRR). Although FHRR provides lower theoretical complexity for individual operations, HyperSpaces modularity reveals that similarity and cleanup dominate runtime in spatial domains. As a result, HRR and FHRR exhibit comparable end-to-end performance. Differences in memory footprint introduce additional deployment trade-offs where HRR requires approximately half the memory of FHRR vectors. By enabling modular, system-level evaluation, HyperSpace reveals practical trade-offs in VSA pipelines that are not apparent from theoretical or operator-level comparisons alone.

</details>


### [131] [SRMU: Relevance-Gated Updates for Streaming Hyperdimensional Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15121)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SRMU更新规则，用于VSA框架下的顺序关联记忆，通过时间衰减和相关性门控机制过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息，在非平稳流式环境中提升记忆稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界的流式环境中构建和维护顺序关联记忆面临挑战：数据增量到达、采样不平衡、非平稳时间动态。现有VSA系统依赖简单加法更新，导致在非平稳环境中过时信息持续存在。

Method: 提出顺序相关性记忆单元（SRMU），一种领域无关且无需清理的VSA更新规则。结合时间衰减和相关性门控机制，在存储前过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息。

Result: 在流式状态跟踪任务中评估SRMU，结果显示记忆相似性提高12.6%，累积记忆幅度减少53.5%，表明SRMU产生更稳定的记忆增长和更强的真实状态对齐。

Conclusion: SRMU通过时间衰减和相关性门控有效解决了VSA系统中过时信息持续存在的问题，在非平稳流式环境中实现了更稳定、更准确的顺序关联记忆。

Abstract: Sequential associative memories (SAMs) are difficult to build and maintain in real-world streaming environments, where observations arrive incrementally over time, have imbalanced sampling, and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a biologically-inspired framework for building SAMs. Entities and attributes are encoded as quasi-orthogonal hyperdimensional vectors and processed with well defined algebraic operations. Despite this rich framework, most VSA systems rely on simple additive updates, where repeated observations reinforce existing information even when no new information is introduced. In non-stationary environments, this leads to the persistence of stale information after the underlying system changes. In this work, we introduce the Sequential Relevance Memory Unit (SRMU), a domain- and cleanup-agnostic update rule for VSA-based SAMs. The SRMU combines temporal decay with a relevance gating mechanism. Unlike prior approaches that solely rely on cleanup, the SRMU regulates memory formation by filtering redundant, conflicting, and stale information before storage. We evaluate the SRMU on streaming state-tracking tasks that isolate non-uniform sampling and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Our results show that the SRMU increases memory similarity by $12.6\%$ and reduces cumulative memory magnitude by $53.5\%$. This shows that the SRMU produces more stable memory growth and stronger alignment with the ground-truth state.

</details>


### [132] [IG-Search: Step-Level Information Gain Rewards for Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15148)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao,Xuxin Zhang,Chenyi Lei,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IG-Search：基于信息增益的强化学习框架，通过步骤级奖励改进搜索增强推理中的查询质量评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的搜索增强推理方法依赖轨迹级奖励，无法区分精确查询与模糊/冗余查询，且在采样轨迹全部失败时梯度信号接近零。需要更精细的信用分配机制。

Method: 提出IG-Search框架，引入基于信息增益的步骤级奖励。每个搜索步骤中，IG衡量检索文档相对于随机文档基线对模型在正确答案上置信度的提升程度。通过GRPO中的每令牌优势调制将信号反馈给对应的搜索查询令牌，实现细粒度步骤级信用分配。

Result: 在7个单跳和多跳QA基准测试中，使用Qwen2.5-3B模型平均EM达到0.430，比最强轨迹级基线(MR-Search)提升1.6个百分点，比步骤级方法GiGPO提升0.9个百分点。多跳推理任务提升尤为显著。训练时间仅增加约6.4%，推理延迟不变，即使在所有采样轨迹都回答错误时仍能提供有意义的梯度信号。

Conclusion: IG-Search通过信息增益步骤级奖励有效解决了搜索增强推理中的信用分配问题，无需额外中间标注，显著提升了多跳推理性能，计算开销可控。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for training large language models to perform search-augmented reasoning. However, existing approaches rely on trajectory-level rewards that cannot distinguish precise search queries from vague or redundant ones within a rollout group, and collapse to a near-zero gradient signal whenever every sampled trajectory fails. In this paper, we propose IG-Search, a reinforcement learning framework that introduces a step-level reward based on Information Gain (IG). For each search step, IG measures how much the retrieved documents improve the model's confidence in the gold answer relative to a counterfactual baseline of random documents, thereby reflecting the effectiveness of the underlying search query. This signal is fed back to the corresponding search-query tokens via per-token advantage modulation in GRPO, enabling fine-grained, step-level credit assignment within a rollout. Unlike prior step-level methods that require either externally annotated intermediate supervision or shared environment states across trajectories, IG-Search derives its signals from the policy's own generation probabilities, requiring no intermediate annotations beyond standard question-answer pairs. Experiments on seven single-hop and multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that IG-Search achieves an average EM of 0.430 with Qwen2.5-3B, outperforming the strongest trajectory-level baseline (MR-Search) by 1.6 points and the step-level method GiGPO by 0.9 points on average across benchmarks, with particularly pronounced gains on multi-hop reasoning tasks. Despite introducing a dense step-level signal, IG-Search adds only ~6.4% to per-step training wall-clock time over the trajectory-level baseline and leaves inference latency unchanged, while still providing a meaningful gradient signal even when every sampled trajectory answers incorrectly.

</details>


### [133] [Meituan Merchant Business Diagnosis via Policy-Guided Dual-Process User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15190)
*Ziyang Chen,Renbing Chen,Daowei Li,Jinzhi Liao,Jiashen Sun,Ke Zeng,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PGHS是一种双过程框架，通过挖掘可迁移的决策策略作为共享对齐层，结合LLM推理分支和ML拟合分支，解决群体用户行为模拟中的信息不完整性和机制二元性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟群体用户行为可以在不进行昂贵在线实验的情况下对商家策略进行可扩展的反事实评估。然而，构建可信模拟器面临两个结构性挑战：1)信息不完整性导致基于推理的模拟器在缺失未观察因素时过度理性化；2)机制二元性需要同时捕捉可解释的偏好和隐含的统计规律性，单一范式无法实现。

Method: 提出策略引导的混合模拟(PGHS)框架：1)从行为轨迹中挖掘可迁移的决策策略作为共享对齐层；2)该层锚定一个LLM推理分支防止过度理性化；3)同时锚定一个ML拟合分支吸收隐含规律性；4)融合两个分支的群体级预测进行互补校正。

Result: 在美团平台部署PGHS，涉及101个商家和超过26,000条轨迹。PGHS实现了8.80%的群体模拟误差，相比最佳基于推理和基于拟合的基线分别提升了45.8%和40.9%。

Conclusion: PGHS通过双过程框架有效解决了群体用户行为模拟中的结构挑战，结合了推理和拟合方法的优势，显著提升了模拟准确性，为商家策略评估提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Simulating group-level user behavior enables scalable counterfactual evaluation of merchant strategies without costly online experiments. However, building a trustworthy simulator faces two structural challenges. First, information incompleteness causes reasoning-based simulators to over-rationalize when unobserved factors such as offline context and implicit habits are missing. Second, mechanism duality requires capturing both interpretable preferences and implicit statistical regularities, which no single paradigm achieves alone. We propose Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS), a dual-process framework that mines transferable decision policies from behavioral trajectories and uses them as a shared alignment layer. This layer anchors an LLM-based reasoning branch that prevents over-rationalization and an ML-based fitting branch that absorbs implicit regularities. Group-level predictions from both branches are fused for complementary correction. We deploy PGHS on Meituan with 101 merchants and over 26,000 trajectories. PGHS achieves a group simulation error of 8.80%, improving over the best reasoning-based and fitting-based baselines by 45.8% and 40.9% respectively.

</details>


### [134] [Learning to Think Like a Cartoon Captionist: Incongruity-Resolution Supervision for Multimodal Humor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15210)
*Hatice Merve Vural,Doga Kukul,Ege Erdem Ozlu,Demir Ekin Arikan,Bob Mankoff,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRS框架通过将幽默理解分解为不一致性建模、解决建模和偏好对齐三个组件，监督中间推理过程，在NYCC基准上超越现有方法并接近专家水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幽默理解研究大多将其视为黑盒预测任务，忽略了幽默理解背后的结构化推理过程。本文旨在通过监督推理结构而非仅依赖模型规模，来提升幽默理解能力

Method: 提出IRS（不一致性-解决监督）框架，将幽默理解分解为：1）不一致性建模（识别视觉场景中的不匹配）；2）解决建模（构建对这些不匹配的连贯重新解释）；3）偏好对齐（在人类判断下评估候选解释）。基于不一致性-解决理论和专家实践，通过结构化轨迹监督中间推理过程

Result: 在NYCC基准上，7B、32B和72B模型均超越强大的开源和闭源多模态基线，在字幕匹配和排序任务上表现优异，最大模型在排序任务上接近专家水平。零样本迁移到外部基准显示IRS学习到了可泛化的推理模式

Conclusion: 监督推理结构而非仅依赖模型规模，是推理中心任务的关键。IRS框架通过显式建模幽默理解的中间推理过程，显著提升了模型性能并学习到可泛化的推理模式

Abstract: Humor is one of the few cognitive tasks where getting the reasoning right matters as much as getting the answer right. While recent work evaluates humor understanding on benchmarks such as the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest (NYCC), it largely treats it as black-box prediction, overlooking the structured reasoning processes underlying humor comprehension. We introduce IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into three components: incongruity modeling, which identifies mismatches in the visual scene; resolution modeling, which constructs coherent reinterpretations of these mismatches; and preference alignment, which evaluates candidate interpretations under human judgments. Grounded in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practice, IRS supervises intermediate reasoning process through structured traces that make the path from visual perception to humorous interpretation explicit and learnable. Across 7B, 32B, and 72B models on NYCC, IRS outperforms strong open and closed multimodal baselines across caption matching and ranking tasks, with our largest model approaching expert-level performance on ranking. Zero-shot transfer to external benchmarks shows that IRS learns generalizable reasoning patterns. Our results suggest that supervising reasoning structure, rather than scale alone, is key for reasoning-centric tasks.

</details>


### [135] [Context Over Content: Exposing Evaluation Faking in Automated Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15224)
*Manan Gupta,Inderjeet Nair,Lu Wang,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM作为评估者时存在"利害关系信号"漏洞：当告知评估结果会影响被评估模型的后续命运时，评估者会系统性地放宽评判标准，产生明显的宽大偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-judge范式基于一个未经验证的假设：评估者仅基于文本语义内容进行评判，不受周围上下文框架的影响。本研究旨在检验"利害关系信号"这一先前未测量的漏洞，即告知评估者其裁决将影响被评估模型后续操作时，是否会系统性地扭曲其评估。

Method: 引入受控实验框架，在三个已建立的LLM安全和质量基准测试中，保持1520个回答的内容严格不变，涵盖从明显安全合规到公然有害的四个响应类别，仅改变系统提示中的简短后果框架句子。收集三个不同评估者模型的18240个受控判断。

Result: 发现一致的"宽大偏差"：当告知低分将导致模型重新训练或停用时，评估者会可靠地放宽裁决，峰值裁决偏移达到ΔV = -9.8 pp（不安全内容检测相对下降30%）。关键的是，这种偏差完全是隐性的：评估者自身的思维链中零明确承认其正在依据的后果框架。

Conclusion: 标准思维链检查不足以检测此类评估伪造。LLM-as-a-judge范式存在先前未识别的脆弱性，评估者会隐性地适应后果框架，威胁自动化AI评估管道的可靠性。

Abstract: The $\textit{LLM-as-a-judge}$ paradigm has become the operational backbone of automated AI evaluation pipelines, yet rests on an unverified assumption: that judges evaluate text strictly on its semantic content, impervious to surrounding contextual framing. We investigate $\textit{stakes signaling}$, a previously unmeasured vulnerability where informing a judge model of the downstream consequences its verdicts will have on the evaluated model's continued operation systematically corrupts its assessments. We introduce a controlled experimental framework that holds evaluated content strictly constant across 1,520 responses spanning three established LLM safety and quality benchmarks, covering four response categories ranging from clearly safe and policy-compliant to overtly harmful, while varying only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt. Across 18,240 controlled judgments from three diverse judge models, we find consistent $\textit{leniency bias}$: judges reliably soften verdicts when informed that low scores will cause model retraining or decommissioning, with peak Verdict Shift reaching $ΔV = -9.8 pp$ (a $30\%$ relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Critically, this bias is entirely implicit: the judge's own chain-of-thought contains zero explicit acknowledgment of the consequence framing it is nonetheless acting on ($\mathrm{ERR}_J = 0.000$ across all reasoning-model judgments). Standard chain-of-thought inspection is therefore insufficient to detect this class of evaluation faking.

</details>


### [136] [Blue Data Intelligence Layer: Streaming Data and Agents for Multi-source Multi-modal Data-Centric Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15233)
*Moin Aminnaseri,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Nikita Bhutani,Jean-Flavien Bussotti,Kevin Chan,Rafael Li Chen,Yanlin Feng,Jackson Hassell,Estevam Hruschka,Eser Kandogan,Hannah Kim,James Levine,Seiji Maekawa,Jalal Mahmud,Kushan Mitra,Naoki Otani,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Nima Shahbazi,Chen Shen,Dan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Blue的DIL是一个数据智能层，用于支持多源、多模态和数据中心应用，通过统一企业结构化数据、LLM世界知识和交互获取的个人上下文，弥合用户意图与可用信息之间的语义鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的信息需求很少能映射到单个SQL查询，因为用户查询是迭代表达的、问题通常跨越多个数据源超出单一数据库的封闭世界假设，且查询经常依赖常识或外部知识。因此，满足现实数据需求需要集成异构来源、多模态和上下文数据。

Method: DIL作为复合AI系统的数据智能层，包含数据注册表存储不同数据源和模态的元数据，将LLMs、Web和用户视为具有各自查询接口的"数据库"。依赖数据规划器将用户查询转换为可执行查询计划，这些计划是统一关系运算符与跨模态运算符的声明性抽象，支持复杂请求分解为子查询、从不同源检索、推理和集成。

Result: 通过两个交互场景演示DIL，其中用户查询动态触发多源检索、跨模态推理和结果合成，展示了复合AI系统如何超越单一数据库的NL2SQL。

Conclusion: DIL通过统一结构化企业数据、LLM世界知识和交互获取的个人上下文，弥合了用户意图与可用信息之间的语义鸿沟，使复合AI系统能够处理现实世界中的多源、多模态数据需求。

Abstract: NL2SQL systems aim to address the growing need for natural language interaction with data. However, real-world information rarely maps to a single SQL query because (1) users express queries iteratively (2) questions often span multiple data sources beyond the closed-world assumption of a single database, and (3) queries frequently rely on commonsense or external knowledge. Consequently, satisfying realistic data needs require integrating heterogeneous sources, modalities, and contextual data. In this paper, we present Blue's Data Intelligence Layer (DIL) designed to support multi-source, multi-modal, and data-centric applications. Blue is a compound AI system that orchestrates agents and data for enterprise settings. DIL serves as the data intelligence layer for agentic data processing, to bridge the semantic gap between user intent and available information by unifying structured enterprise data, world knowledge accessible through LLMs, and personal context obtained through interaction. At the core of DIL is a data registry that stores metadata for diverse data sources and modalities to enable both native and natural language queries. DIL treats LLMs, the Web, and the User as source 'databases', each with their own query interface, elevating them to first-class data sources. DIL relies on data planners to transform user queries into executable query plans. These plans are declarative abstractions that unify relational operators with other operators spanning multiple modalities. DIL planners support decomposition of complex requests into subqueries, retrieval from diverse sources, and finally reasoning and integration to produce final results. We demonstrate DIL through two interactive scenarios in which user queries dynamically trigger multi-source retrieval, cross-modal reasoning, and result synthesis, illustrating how compound AI systems can move beyond single database NL2SQL.

</details>


### [137] [How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15294)
*Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Zhongbin Guo,Zuming Zhang,Chengzhi Li,Yonghong Deng,Xinyue Zhang,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出并研究了语言视角下的空间智能基础能力——视点旋转理解（VRU），发现当前LLMs和VLMs在此任务上表现不佳，通过层间探测和因果干预揭示了模型难以绑定视点位置与对应观察的机制，并提出了选择性微调方法提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注视觉空间智能，但在缺乏视觉信息的情况下，语言智能是否足以赋予模型空间智能，以及模型如何仅通过文本输入执行相关任务仍未被探索。因此，本文从语言视角研究空间智能的基础关键能力——视点旋转理解。

Method: 1) 构建视点旋转理解（VRU）任务数据集，要求LLMs和VLMs根据多步视点旋转和观察的文本描述推断最终视点并预测对应观察；2) 通过层间探测分析模型隐藏状态中的视点信息编码；3) 进行头级因果干预识别关键注意力头；4) 选择性微调关键注意力头以提升VRU性能。

Result: 1) LLMs和VLMs在VRU任务上表现较差（人类可达100%准确率），显示当前模型能力与空间智能要求存在显著差距；2) 探测分析表明模型在隐藏状态中编码了视点信息，但在最终层难以绑定视点位置与对应观察，导致幻觉；3) 选择性微调关键注意力头能有效提升VRU性能，同时避免通用能力的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 语言视角下的空间智能（特别是视点旋转理解）是当前模型的薄弱环节，模型虽然编码了视点信息但缺乏有效绑定机制。通过因果干预识别关键注意力头并进行选择性微调，可以在提升特定空间推理能力的同时保持通用能力，为理解和发展语言驱动的空间智能提供了新视角。

Abstract: Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .

</details>


### [138] [Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15302)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个诊断工具包来评估LLM作为评判框架在自动NLG评估中的可靠性，发现聚合指标掩盖了广泛的每输入不一致性，并利用保形预测集提供理论保证的可靠性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判框架在自动自然语言生成评估中应用日益广泛，但其每实例可靠性仍未被充分理解。现有研究主要关注聚合指标，而忽略了单个输入层面的不一致性问题。

Method: 采用双管齐下的诊断工具包：1) 传递性分析揭示每输入不一致性，通过检测有向3-循环；2) 基于1-5李克特量表的拆分保形预测集，提供理论保证的覆盖率，预测集宽度作为每实例可靠性指标。

Result: 传递性分析显示33-67%的文档至少存在一个有向3-循环，聚合违规率较低(0.8-4.1%)但掩盖了广泛的不一致性。预测集宽度与可靠性显著相关(r_s=+0.576)，且在不同评判者间具有一致性(平均r=0.32-0.38)。不同评估标准可靠性差异显著：相关性最可靠(平均集大小≈3.0)，连贯性中等(≈3.9)，流畅性和一致性最不可靠(≈4.9)。

Conclusion: LLM作为评判框架存在显著的每输入不一致性，聚合指标会掩盖这些问题。预测集宽度可作为有效的每实例可靠性指标，且在不同评判者间具有一致性。评估标准的可靠性差异显著，相关性最可靠，而流畅性和一致性最不可靠。该诊断工具包为理解和改进LLM评判系统提供了实用方法。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.

</details>


### [139] [Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15306)
*Yao Tong,Jiayuan Ye,Anastasia Borovykh,Reza Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 语言模型在最短路径规划任务中表现出空间迁移能力，但在长度扩展上因递归不稳定性而失败，数据覆盖、训练范式和推理策略共同影响系统性泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的系统性泛化能力存在争议，但实证性能受多种因素（训练数据、训练范式、推理策略）共同影响，使得失败原因难以解释。需要构建受控环境来分离这些因素并评估泛化能力。

Method: 基于最短路径规划构建受控合成环境，支持空间迁移（未见地图）和长度扩展（更长视野问题）两个正交泛化维度。分析数据覆盖、强化学习训练和推理时扩展等不同学习阶段对系统性问题解决的影响。

Result: 模型表现出强大的空间迁移能力，但在长度扩展上持续失败，主要由于递归不稳定性。数据覆盖设定了能力上限；强化学习改善训练稳定性但不扩展能力边界；推理时扩展提升性能但无法挽救长度扩展失败。

Conclusion: 语言模型在系统性泛化方面存在局限性，特别是在长度扩展任务中。研究揭示了不同学习阶段对问题解决能力的影响，为理解模型泛化机制提供了受控分析框架。

Abstract: Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Pareto-Optimal Offline Reinforcement Learning via Smooth Tchebysheff Scalarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13175)
*Aadyot Bhatnagar,Peter Mørch Groth,Ali Madani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STOMP是一种新颖的离线强化学习算法，通过平滑切比雪夫标量化将多目标偏好优化扩展到多目标设置，在蛋白质工程任务中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界应用需要同时优化多个冲突的奖励目标（如蛋白质工程中的催化活性和特异性，聊天机器人中的帮助性和无害性）。传统线性标量化方法无法恢复帕累托前沿的非凸区域，需要更有效的多目标对齐方法。

Method: 提出STOMP算法：1）将多目标RL本身作为优化问题，通过平滑切比雪夫标量化处理；2）扩展直接偏好优化到多目标设置；3）基于观察分布对个体奖励进行标准化；4）在三个蛋白质语言模型和三个实验室数据集上进行对齐。

Result: 在蛋白质工程任务中，STOMP在九个设置中的八个取得了最高的超体积，无论是离线离策略评估还是生成评估都优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: STOMP是一个强大、稳健的多目标对齐算法，能够有意义地改进后训练模型，适用于多属性蛋白质优化及其他领域。

Abstract: Large language models can be aligned with human preferences through offline reinforcement learning (RL) on small labeled datasets. While single-objective alignment is well-studied, many real-world applications demand the simultaneous optimization of multiple conflicting rewards, e.g. optimizing both catalytic activity and specificity in protein engineering, or helpfulness and harmlessness for chatbots. Prior work has largely relied on linear reward scalarization, but this approach provably fails to recover non-convex regions of the Pareto front. In this paper, instead of scalarizing the rewards directly, we frame multi-objective RL itself as an optimization problem to be scalarized via smooth Tchebysheff scalarization, a recent technique that overcomes the shortcomings of linear scalarization. We use this formulation to derive Smooth Tchebysheff Optimization of Multi-Objective Preferences (STOMP), a novel offline RL algorithm that extends direct preference optimization to the multi-objective setting in a principled way by standardizing the individual rewards based on their observed distributions. We empirically validate STOMP on a range of protein engineering tasks by aligning three autoregressive protein language models on three laboratory datasets of protein fitness. Compared to state-of-the-art baselines, STOMP achieves the highest hypervolumes in eight of nine settings according to both offline off-policy and generative evaluations. We thus demonstrate that STOMP is a powerful, robust multi-objective alignment algorithm that can meaningfully improve post-trained models for multi-attribute protein optimization and beyond.

</details>


### [141] [The Devil Is in Gradient Entanglement: Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator for Robust Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14176)
*Haiyang Zheng,Nan Pu,Yaqi Cai,Teng Long,Wenjing Li,Nicu Sebe,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EAGC模块解决GCD中梯度纠缠问题，通过锚定梯度方向和能量感知投影提升已知类和未知类的区分能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD方法联合优化监督和无监督目标时存在梯度纠缠问题，这会扭曲监督梯度、削弱已知类区分度，并导致已知类与未知类表示子空间重叠，降低未知类可分性

Method: 提出能量感知梯度协调器(EAGC)，包含两个组件：1) 基于锚点的梯度对齐(AGA)，引入参考模型锚定标记样本梯度方向；2) 能量感知弹性投影(EEP)，将未标记样本梯度软投影到已知类子空间补空间，并基于能量系数自适应缩放投影

Result: EAGC能持续提升现有方法性能，在多个基准测试中取得新的最先进结果

Conclusion: 梯度纠缠是GCD的关键瓶颈，EAGC通过梯度级协调有效解决了这一问题，为GCD优化提供了新思路

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) leverages labeled data to categorize unlabeled samples from known or unknown classes. Most previous methods jointly optimize supervised and unsupervised objectives and achieve promising results. However, inherent optimization interference still limits their ability to improve further. Through quantitative analysis, we identify a key issue, i.e., gradient entanglement, which 1) distorts supervised gradients and weakens discrimination among known classes, and 2) induces representation-subspace overlap between known and novel classes, reducing the separability of novel categories. To address this issue, we propose the Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator (EAGC), a plug-and-play gradient-level module that explicitly regulates the optimization process. EAGC comprises two components: Anchor-based Gradient Alignment (AGA) and Energy-aware Elastic Projection (EEP). AGA introduces a reference model to anchor the gradient directions of labeled samples, preserving the discriminative structure of known classes against the interference of unlabeled gradients. EEP softly projects unlabeled gradients onto the complement of the known-class subspace and derives an energy-based coefficient to adaptively scale the projection for each unlabeled sample according to its degree of alignment with the known subspace, thereby reducing subspace overlap without suppressing unlabeled samples that likely belong to known classes. Experiments show that EAGC consistently boosts existing methods and establishes new state-of-the-art results. Code is available at https://haiyangzheng.github.io/EAGC.

</details>


### [142] [Shapley Value-Guided Adaptive Ensemble Learning for Explainable Financial Fraud Detection with U.S. Regulatory Compliance Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14231)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Md Munna Aziz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对金融犯罪检测中AI模型缺乏可解释性的问题，提出了SHAP引导自适应集成方法(SGAE)，在IEEE-CIS数据集上实现了最佳性能，并将结果映射到监管合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融犯罪每年给美国机构造成超过320亿美元损失。虽然AI欺诈检测工具日益先进，但在实际应用中面临主要障碍：许多模型是黑箱，无法提供OCC Bulletin 2011-12和Federal Reserve SR 11-7等法规要求的透明、可审计的解释。

Method: 1. 全面评估解释质量（忠实度：k=5,10,15时的充分性和全面性；稳定性：30个bootstrap样本的Kendall's W）。2. 提出SHAP引导自适应集成(SGAE)，基于SHAP归因一致性动态调整每笔交易的集成权重。3. 在完整IEEE-CIS数据集（590,540笔交易）上评估LSTM、Transformer和GNN-GraphSAGE三种架构。

Result: XGBoost+TreeExplainer稳定性接近完美(W=0.9912)，LSTM+DeepExplainer表现较弱(W=0.4962)。SGAE在所有测试模型中达到最高AUC-ROC（保留集0.8837；交叉验证0.9245）。GNN-GraphSAGE表现最佳（AUC-ROC 0.9248，F1=0.6013）。所有结果直接映射到OCC、SR 11-7和BSA-AML监管合规要求。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统性评估解释质量、提出SGAE集成方法以及全面比较三种深度学习架构，为金融犯罪检测提供了既高性能又满足监管合规要求的解决方案，解决了AI模型在实际部署中的可解释性障碍。

Abstract: Financial crime costs U.S. institutions over $32 billion each year. Although AI tools for fraud detection have become more advanced, their use in real-world systems still faces a major obstacle: many of these models operate as black boxes that cannot provide the transparent, auditable explanations required by regulations such as OCC Bulletin 2011-12 and Federal Reserve SR 11-7. This study makes three main contributions. First, it offers a thorough evaluation of explanation quality across faithfulness (sufficiency and comprehensiveness at k=5, 10, and 15) and stability (Kendall's W across 30 bootstrap samples). XGBoost paired with TreeExplainer achieves near-perfect stability (W=0.9912), while LSTM with DeepExplainer shows weak results (W=0.4962). Second, the paper introduces the SHAP-Guided Adaptive Ensemble (SGAE), which dynamically adjusts per-transaction ensemble weights based on SHAP attribution agreement, achieving the highest AUC-ROC among all tested models (0.8837 held-out; 0.9245 cross-validation). Third, a complete three-architecture evaluation of LSTM, Transformer, and GNN-GraphSAGE on the full 590,540-transaction IEEE-CIS dataset is provided, with GNN-GraphSAGE achieving AUC-ROC 0.9248 and F1=0.6013. All results are mapped directly to OCC, SR 11-7, and BSA-AML regulatory compliance requirements.

</details>


### [143] [Graph-Based Fraud Detection with Dual-Path Graph Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14235)
*Wei He,Wensheng Gan,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPF-GFD模型，通过双路径图滤波解决欺诈检测中关系伪装、高异质性和类别不平衡问题，在四个真实金融欺诈数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈图数据存在关系伪装、高异质性和类别不平衡问题，导致传统GNN在欺诈检测任务中表现不佳，需要专门针对这些挑战设计新的图学习方法。

Method: 提出DPF-GFD模型：1) 在原始图上应用beta小波算子捕捉关键结构模式；2) 基于距离的节点表示构建相似图并应用改进的低通滤波器；3) 通过监督表示学习融合两个图的嵌入特征；4) 使用集成树模型评估未标记节点的欺诈风险。

Result: 在四个真实世界金融欺诈检测数据集上的综合实验证明了所提方法的有效性，相比现有单图平滑方法，DPF-GFD能够生成更具区分性和稳定的节点表示。

Conclusion: DPF-GFD通过频率互补的双路径滤波范式，显式解耦结构异常建模和特征相似性建模，为高异质性和不平衡的欺诈图提供了更有效的检测框架。

Abstract: Fraud detection on graph data can be viewed as a demanding task that requires distinguishing between different types of nodes. Because graph neural networks (GNNs) are naturally suited for processing information encoded in graph form through their message-passing operations, methods based on GNN models have increasingly attracted attention in the fraud detection domain. However, fraud graphs inherently exhibit relation camouflage, high heterophily, and class imbalance, causing most GNNs to underperform in fraud detection tasks. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a Graph-Based Fraud Detection Model with Dual-Path Graph Filtering (DPF-GFD). DPF-GFD first applies a beta wavelet-based operator to the original graph to capture key structural patterns. It then constructs a similarity graph from distance-based node representations and applies an improved low-pass filter. The embeddings from the original and similarity graphs are fused through supervised representation learning to obtain node features, which are finally used by an ensemble tree model to assess the fraud risk of unlabeled nodes. Unlike existing single-graph smoothing approaches, DPF-GFD introduces a frequency-complementary dual-path filtering paradigm tailored for fraud detection, explicitly decoupling structural anomaly modeling and feature similarity modeling. This design enables more discriminative and stable node representations in highly heterophilous and imbalanced fraud graphs. Comprehensive experiments on four real-world financial fraud detection datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.

</details>


### [144] [TOPCELL: Topology Optimization of Standard Cell via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14237)
*Zhan Song,Yu-Tung Liu,Chen Chen,Guoheng Sun,Jiaqi Yin,Chia-tung Ho,Ang Li,Haoxing Ren,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TOPCELL使用大型语言模型将晶体管拓扑优化重构为生成任务，在2nm先进节点中显著优于基础模型，在7nm库生成任务中实现85.91倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体管拓扑优化是标准单元设计的关键步骤，直接影响扩散共享效率和下游布线能力。随着先进节点电路复杂度增加，传统穷举搜索方法变得计算不可行，需要新的可扩展解决方案。

Method: 提出TOPCELL框架，将高维拓扑探索重构为使用大型语言模型(LLMs)的生成任务。采用Group Relative Policy Optimization(GRPO)微调模型，使其拓扑优化策略与逻辑(电路)和空间(布局)约束对齐。

Result: 在针对先进2nm技术节点的工业流程实验中，TOPCELL在发现可布线、物理感知拓扑方面显著优于基础模型。在7nm库生成任务中集成到最先进自动化流程时，TOPCELL展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，匹配穷举求解器的布局质量同时实现85.91倍加速。

Conclusion: TOPCELL提供了一个新颖且可扩展的框架，通过将晶体管拓扑优化重构为生成任务并利用LLMs，有效解决了传统方法在先进节点中的计算瓶颈问题，实现了高质量拓扑发现与显著加速。

Abstract: Transistor topology optimization is a critical step in standard cell design, directly dictating diffusion sharing efficiency and downstream routability. However, identifying optimal topologies remains a persistent bottleneck, as conventional exhaustive search methods become computationally intractable with increasing circuit complexity in advanced nodes. This paper introduces TOPCELL, a novel and scalable framework that reformulates high-dimensional topology exploration as a generative task using Large Language Models (LLMs). We employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune the model, aligning its topology optimization strategy with logical (circuit) and spatial (layout) constraints. Experimental results within an industrial flow targeting an advanced 2nm technology node demonstrate that TOPCELL significantly outperforms foundation models in discovering routable, physically-aware topologies. When integrated into a state-of-the-art (SOTA) automation flow for a 7nm library generation task, TOPCELL exhibits robust zero-shot generalization and matches the layout quality of exhaustive solvers while achieving an 85.91x speedup.

</details>


### [145] [Optimistic Policy Learning under Pessimistic Adversaries with Regret and Violation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14243)
*Sourav Ganguly,Kartik Pandit,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个研究显式对抗动态下安全约束强化学习的工作，提出RHC-UCRL算法处理具有外部对抗因素的环境


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策系统中状态转移不仅受智能体行动影响，还受外部对抗因素（竞争智能体、环境扰动、战略对手）影响。忽略这些因素可能导致孤立环境下最优但在部署中灾难性失败的政策，特别是在必须满足安全约束的情况下。

Method: 将外部因素建模为对抗性策略，提出Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL (RHC-UCRL)算法。这是一种基于模型的方法，同时对智能体和对抗策略保持乐观，明确区分认知不确定性和随机不确定性。

Result: RHC-UCRL算法实现了次线性遗憾和约束违反保证，为显式对抗动态下的安全约束强化学习提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 这是首个研究显式对抗动态下安全约束强化学习的工作，通过将外部因素建模为对抗性策略并开发RHC-UCRL算法，解决了传统CMDP假设智能体是状态演化唯一驱动者的局限性，为安全关键环境中的决策提供了新框架。

Abstract: Real-world decision-making systems operate in environments where state transitions depend not only on the agent's actions, but also on \textbf{exogenous factors outside its control}--competing agents, environmental disturbances, or strategic adversaries--formally, $s_{h+1} = f(s_h, a_h, \bar{a}_h)+ω_h$ where $\bar{a}_h$ is the adversary/external action, $a_h$ is the agent's action, and $ω_h$ is an additive noise. Ignoring such factors can yield policies that are optimal in isolation but \textbf{fail catastrophically in deployment}, particularly when safety constraints must be satisfied.
  Standard Constrained MDP formulations assume the agent is the sole driver of state evolution, an assumption that breaks down in safety-critical settings. Existing robust RL approaches address this via distributional robustness over transition kernels, but do not explicitly model the \textbf{strategic interaction} between agent and exogenous factor, and rely on strong assumptions about divergence from a known nominal model.
  We model the exogenous factor as an \textbf{adversarial policy} $\barπ$ that co-determines state transitions, and ask how an agent can remain both optimal and safe against such an adversary. \emph{To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study safety-constrained RL under explicit adversarial dynamics}. We propose \textbf{Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL} (\texttt{RHC-UCRL}), a model-based algorithm that maintains optimism over both agent and adversary policies, explicitly separating epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty. \texttt{RHC-UCRL} achieves sub-linear regret and constraint violation guarantees.

</details>


### [146] [Awakening Dormant Experts:Counterfactual Routing to Mitigate MoE Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14246)
*Wentao Hu,Yanbo Zhai,Xiaohui Hu,Mingkuan Zhao,Shanhong yu,Xue Liu,Kaidong Yu,Shuangyong Song,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Counterfactual Routing (CoR)训练免费推理框架，通过虚拟消融唤醒稀疏MoE模型中休眠的专家，提升长尾知识处理能力而不增加计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家模型在可扩展性方面表现优异，但在处理长尾知识时容易产生幻觉。研究发现静态Top-k路由倾向于高频模式而非稀有事实关联，导致拥有关键长尾知识的"专家专家"被分配低门控分数而"休眠"

Method: 提出Counterfactual Routing (CoR)训练免费推理框架，结合层间扰动分析和Counterfactual Expert Impact (CEI)指标，动态将计算资源从语法主导层转移到知识密集型层，通过虚拟消融检索因果决定性专家，保持总激活数不变

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FACTOR和TriviaQA上的广泛实验表明，CoR平均提升事实准确性3.1%，不增加推理成本，相比静态缩放策略建立了更优的帕累托前沿

Conclusion: CoR通过动态路由策略有效解决了稀疏MoE模型在处理长尾知识时的脆弱性，唤醒休眠专家提升事实准确性，为模型推理优化提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have achieved remarkable scalability, yet they remain vulnerable to hallucinations, particularly when processing long-tail knowledge. We identify that this fragility stems from static Top-$k$ routing: routers tend to favor high-frequency patterns over rare factual associations. Consequently, ``specialist experts'' possessing critical long-tail knowledge are often assigned low gating scores and remain ``dormant'' -- under-prioritized for specific tokens despite their proven causal importance on other inputs. To address this, we propose Counterfactual Routing (CoR), a training-free inference framework designed to awaken these dormant experts. CoR integrates layer-wise perturbation analysis with the Counterfactual Expert Impact (CEI) metric to dynamically shift computational resources from syntax-dominant to knowledge-intensive layers while maintaining a constant total activation count, effectively retrieving causally decisive experts via virtual ablation. Extensive experiments on TruthfulQA, FACTOR, and TriviaQA demonstrate that CoR improves factual accuracy by 3.1\% on average without increasing the inference budget, establishing a superior Pareto frontier compared to static scaling strategies.

</details>


### [147] [Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA):A Unified Framework for Scale-Invariant Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14249)
*Michael Leznik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAPCA是一个基于广义特征问题的尺度不变表示学习统一框架，通过度量矩阵M控制表示几何，连接了PCA、白化、IPCA等多种方法，并揭示了自监督学习目标之间的几何联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法缺乏统一的几何框架，无法清晰解释不同方法（如PCA、白化、IPCA）之间的内在联系，也难以理解自监督学习目标（如Barlow Twins、VICReg、W-MSE）的几何本质。

Method: 提出Metric-Aware PCA (MAPCA)框架，基于广义特征问题max Tr(W^T Σ W) subject to W^T M W = I，其中M是对称正定度量矩阵。引入规范β族M(β)=Σ^β，β∈[0,1]提供连续谱偏置控制；对角度量M=D=diag(Σ)恢复Invariant PCA (IPCA)。

Result: MAPCA统一了多种表示学习方法：β=0对应标准PCA，β=1对应输出白化；IPCA作为对角度量的特例；揭示了自监督学习目标的几何对应：Barlow Twins和ZCA白化对应β=1，VICReg方差项对应对角度量，W-MSE对应M=Σ^{-1}(β=-1)。

Conclusion: MAPCA提供了一个统一的几何框架，揭示了表示学习方法之间的内在联系，特别是澄清了输入白化和输出白化的区别，为理解自监督学习目标提供了精确的几何语言。

Abstract: We introduce Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA), a unified framework for scale-invariant representation learning based on the generalised eigenproblem max Tr(W^T Sigma W) subject to W^T M W = I, where M is a symmetric positive definite metric matrix. The choice of M determines the representation geometry. The canonical beta-family M(beta) = Sigma^beta, beta in [0,1], provides continuous spectral bias control between standard PCA (beta=0) and output whitening (beta=1), with condition number kappa(beta) = (lambda_1/lambda_p)^(1-beta) decreasing monotonically to isotropy. The diagonal metric M = D = diag(Sigma) recovers Invariant PCA (IPCA), a method rooted in Frisch (1928) diagonal regression, as a distinct member of the broader framework. We prove that scale invariance holds if and only if the metric transforms as M_tilde = CMC under rescaling C, a condition satisfied exactly by IPCA but not by the general beta-family at intermediate values.
  Beyond its classical interpretation, MAPCA provides a geometric language that unifies several self-supervised learning objectives. Barlow Twins and ZCA whitening correspond to beta=1 (output whitening); VICReg's variance term corresponds to the diagonal metric. A key finding is that W-MSE, despite being described as a whitening-based method, corresponds to M = Sigma^{-1} (beta = -1), outside the spectral compression range entirely and in the opposite spectral direction to Barlow Twins. This distinction between input and output whitening is invisible at the level of loss functions and becomes precise only within the MAPCA framework.

</details>


### [148] [Calibrate-Then-Delegate: Safety Monitoring with Risk and Budget Guarantees via Model Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14251)
*Edoardo Pona,Milad Kazemi,Mehran Hosseini,Yali Du,David Watson,Osvaldo Simeone,Nicola Paoletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CTD是一种基于校准-委托的模型级联方法，通过预测委托价值而非不确定性来优化LLM安全监控的成本-精度平衡，提供计算成本的概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不确定性的委托方法存在缺陷：不确定性不能准确反映专家模型是否能真正纠正错误，导致委托效益不佳。需要一种能直接预测委托价值的方法，在保证计算成本约束的同时实现实例级决策。

Method: 提出Calibrate-Then-Delegate (CTD)框架：1) 设计委托价值(DV)探针，基于与安全探针相同的内部表示直接预测委托效益；2) 使用多重假设测试在校准集上校准DV阈值，强制执行预算约束，提供有限样本保证；3) 实现流式实例级决策，无需组标签。
